# PE — Peru sights: individual worldwide reviews

[Worldwide v2](../../../rules.md) · [Workflow](../../future-country-workflow.md). Initial editorial inventory, 2026-11-25. All 13 inscribed UNESCO properties represented at property level, not every serial component or tentative property. Chan Chan remains on the List of World Heritage in Danger (https://whc.unesco.org/en/decisions/9024). Coordinates approximate and not independently verified. Qhapaq Ñan reference point is not a confirmed inscribed component. Search-result references support identity or context only. Shared peer sets require closer same-type calibration, particularly archaeology, wetlands and high mountains. Access, safety, community consent and permits not guaranteed.

### Pass 255 scope decisions

16 additions, 11 source-linked provisional, 5 desk-based provisional, 10 90+, 43 ranking-row comparisons (14 international). PE 80 ranked / 82 total, +16 since baseline 66. Peru selected for major archaeological and cultural omissions. Inti Raymi and other celebrations separated from permanent host sites; Qoyllur Rit’i limited to community-permitted participation. Choquequirao site and approach grouped; Salkantay excludes Machu Picchu destination and Humantay detour; Ausangate excludes Rainbow Mountain and pilgrimage. El Brujo and Cao Museum grouped. Q’eswachaka renewal (https://ich.unesco.org/en/decisions/8.COM/8.22) deferred pending public visitor scope and overlap with Qhapaq Ñan. Existing scores and two unassessed retained. Coordinates approximate; access/schedules unverified. Discovery queue open; further UNESCO, reserves, regional museums and festival screen pending.

### Pass 256 scope decisions

16 additions, 13 source-linked provisional, 3 desk-based provisional, 8 90+, 40 ranking-row comparisons (4 international). PE 96 ranked / 98 total, +32 since baseline. All 13 inscribed UNESCO properties already represented, Río Abiseo unassessed; UNESCO reports 25 tentative properties (https://whc.unesco.org/en/statesparties/pe), not all represented. New full or partial tentative-property coverage added; no claim of complete tentative coverage. Remaining screen: Santa Bárbara mining, Collao churches, Utcubamba aggregate scope, pisco vineyards, Huaringas, Central Railway, Sondondo, fossil basins, Sierra del Divisor, La Leche, guano reserve, Lomas, expanded Chankillo and other tentative components. Q’eswachaka annual renewal deferral resolved via documented regional tourism promotion and separate event scope; permanent bridge stays within Qhapaq Ñan. Rural Baroque circuit excludes Cusco core; Cantalloc and Lachay represent partial tentative sites only. Inkaterra canopy is adjacent to Tambopata, not a second ranking for same reserve. Coordinates approximate; access/schedules unverified. Existing scores and two unassessed preserved. Discovery queue open.

### Pass 257 scope decisions

14 additions, 12 source-linked provisional, 2 desk-based provisional, 7 90+, 36 ranking-row comparisons (8 international). PE 110 ranked / 112 total, +46 since baseline. Central Railway 2026 tourist departures cancelled per operator, no future availability assumed; intrinsic established journey retained. Andean Explorer corridor grouped, off-train attractions non-additive. Ica pisco, Palomino and Sacaco partial tentative coverage only. Huaringas community-permitted cultural landscape, no medicinal claims. Santa Bárbara mining deferred pending safe visitor scope and environmental remediation (https://www.digesa.minsa.gob.pe/noticias/Julio2026/nota35.asp); full fossil basin and Ocucaje field access not asserted (https://whc.unesco.org/en/tentativelists/6418/). Remaining tentative screen: Collao churches, Utcubamba, Sierra del Divisor, La Leche, expanded Chankillo and remaining Lomas components. Prior UNESCO inscribed representation finding retained; not independently repeated. Existing scores and two unassessed preserved. Coordinates approximate; access/schedules unverified except documented rail cancellation. Discovery queue open.

### Pass 258 scope decisions

13 additions, 10 source-linked provisional, 3 desk-based provisional, 5 90+, 31 domestic ranking-row comparisons (0 international this pass; international calibration pending). PE 123 ranked / 125 total, +59 since baseline. Collao Juli–Pomata partial serial coverage; Utcubamba separate component experiences, no duplicate aggregate. Cerro Sechín distinct from currently inscribed Chankillo; proposed 11-component 2026 extension (https://whc.unesco.org/en/tentativelists/7055/) must not be separately aggregated. Huánuco Pampa and Aypate deferred as Qhapaq Ñan components (https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1459/maps/ and https://whc.unesco.org/en/documents/209587). Vilcashuamán limited to central site, possible road-system boundary overlap pending. La Leche tentative aggregate overlaps Bosque de Pómac; residual Huaca Lucía–Chólope, Huaca Soledad and historic productive complexes deferred until discrete visitor offering documented (https://whc.unesco.org/en/tentativelists/6419/). Sierra del Divisor deferred: park zoning and Contamana access inventory do not establish a distinct authorized park visitor circuit, no access assumption (https://www.gob.pe/institucion/sernanp/normas-legales/7424609-115-2025-sernanp and https://consultasenlinea.mincetur.gob.pe/fichaInventario/index.aspx?cod_Ficha=12551). Prior mining and fossil deferrals retained. Existing scores and two unassessed preserved. Coordinates approximate; access/schedules unverified. Discovery queue open; remaining tentative and regional screen needed.

### Pass 259 scope decisions

14 additions, 11 source-linked provisional, 3 desk-based provisional, 6 90+, 35 ranking-row comparisons (5 international). PE 137 ranked / 139 total, +73 since baseline. Bahuaja Sonene distinct authorized sectors from Tambopata; Nor Yauyos grouped waterfalls, lakes and villages, excludes Qhapaq Ñan components. Club Libertad Marinera 2026 in Callao, venue variable; separate Trujillo municipal competition deferred pending recurring scope (https://www.gob.pe/institucion/munitrujillo/noticias/1358835-la-marinera-regreso-a-trujillo-con-un-coliseo-gran-chimu-lleno-de-emocion-y-tradicion). San Juan and Amazon Carnival separate. Pampa Hermosa previous temporary trail closure and Ampay limited visitor days documented. Megantoni/Pongo de Mainique deferred pending authorized community-permitted visitor circuit (https://www.gob.pe/institucion/sernanp/informes-publicaciones/1928755-santuario-nacional-megantoni and https://consultasenlinea.mincetur.gob.pe/fichaInventario/index.aspx?cod_Ficha=14381). Prior deferrals retained; no claim of complete park or tentative coverage. Existing scores and two unassessed retained. Coordinates approximate, access/schedules unverified. Queue open for remaining regional and protected-area screen.

### Pass 260 scope decisions

13 additions, 8 source-linked provisional, 5 desk-based provisional, 5 90+, 31 ranking-row comparisons (4 international). PE 150 ranked / 152 total, +86 since baseline. UNESCO country list rechecked (https://whc.unesco.org/en/statesparties/pe): all 13 inscribed properties represented (Río Abiseo unassessed); 23 of 25 tentative properties represented at least in part, not complete serial coverage. Santa Bárbara and Sierra del Divisor wholly deferred; La Leche, Chankillo extension, rural temples, Collao, Utcubamba, pisco, fossil basins, Nasca aqueducts, guano islands and coastal lomas residual scope unresolved. Andagua circuit excludes already-ranked Colca; culinary experience limited to Surquillo market and cooking, UNESCO designation applies to practice not market. Whale circuit excludes beach recreation; Huaylarsh route grouped. Tren Macho distinct from Central Railway, suspension documented, no reopening assumed. All earlier deferrals retained; protected-area screen not exhaustive. Given five additional 90+ omissions in this pass, keep queue open for one further targeted screen (northern archaeology, mountain routes, Amazon birding, cultural experiences), rather than close prematurely. Existing scores and two unassessed preserved. Coordinates approximate; access/schedules unverified.

### Pass 261 scope decisions

12 additions, 7 source-linked provisional, 5 desk-based provisional, 5 90+, 42 ranking-row comparisons (18 international). PE 162 ranked / 164 total, +98 since baseline 66. Abra Patricia and Huembo distinct reserves; Gran Vilaya excludes Kuélap, Gocta, Sonche and Pueblo de los Muertos; Cedros–Alpamayo excludes Santa Cruz segments and is non-additive to Huascarán. Spring and harvest festivals distinct from permanent heritage and existing competition/distillery entries. Existing scores and two unassessed preserved. Earlier UNESCO inventory 13/13 inscribed and 23/25 tentative partially represented carried forward, not rechecked. Sechín Bajo and other Chankillo serial extensions deferred pending visitor-scope evidence; prior Santa Bárbara, Sierra del Divisor, Megantoni and other deferrals retained. Queue remains OPEN for targeted regional coverage after additional high-value omissions. Coordinates approximate; current access and schedules unverified.

12 additions, 6 source-linked provisional, 6 desk-based provisional, 3 90+, 38 ranking-row comparisons (14 international). PE 174 ranked / 176 total, +110 since baseline 66. Áspero and Vichama grouped as separate coastal visit circuit excluding Sacred City of Caral; no UNESCO extension claimed. Peñico deferred for separate scope screen. Raymi Llaqta national-interest legislation is not final designation; Otuzco national heritage not UNESCO inscription. Alto Mayo forest deferred because Abra Patricia overlap is documented; non-overlapping routes require evidence. Villa Rica coffee distinct from wilderness. Alto Shilcayo excludes Ahuashiyacu, Quinua excludes battlefield, community art consent required. Prior UNESCO 13/13 inscribed and 23/25 tentative partially represented carried forward, not rechecked. Previous deferrals preserved. Current queue remains OPEN after three additional 90+ omissions, next screen Peñico, remaining regional heritage and authorized protected-area visitor experiences. Existing scores and two unassessed preserved. Coordinates approximate; current access and schedules unverified.

### Pass 263 scope decisions

12 additions, 11 source-linked provisional, 1 desk-based provisional, 3 90+, 35 ranking-row comparisons (9 international). PE 186 ranked / 188 total, +122 since baseline 66. Peñico independently visitable per official 2025 opening; distinct from Caral and Áspero/Vichama, no extension of UNESCO status. Huánuco and Apurímac festivals restricted to named regional events. Junín birding requires prior coordination; Tingana wetland excludes Abra Patricia and Waqanki. Punta Coles and Guañape represent separate guano reserve sectors; no landing at Guañape, incomplete serial coverage. Prior UNESCO 13/13 inscribed and 23/25 tentative partially represented carried forward, not rechecked. Amarakaeri and other restricted reserves remain deferred pending authorized visitor scope; Calipuy sanctuary and reserve need distinct visitor-route confirmation. Prior deferrals retained. Keep queue OPEN for final targeted screen of Calipuy, distinct Amazon community experiences and regional cultural omissions. Existing scores and two unassessed preserved. Coordinates approximate, schedules and current access unverified.

### Pass 264 scope decisions

12 additions, 11 source-linked provisional, 1 desk-based provisional, 4 90+, 40 ranking-row comparisons (16 international). PE 198 ranked / 200 total, +134 since baseline 66. Corpus Christi, Santurantikuy and Señor de los Temblores are distinct recurring events, no overlapping Cusco Holy Week umbrella. Raqchi and Chinchero combine archaeology and community experiences, exclude Qhapaq Ñan mileage. Calipuy sanctuary and reserve separate visitor scopes. Puerto Prado distinct from Pacaya Samiria; Infierno deferred because SERNANP explicitly includes it in Tambopata visitor scope (https://visitaareasnaturales.sernanp.gob.pe/anps/reserva-nacional-tambopata/). Earlier UNESCO 13/13 inscribed and 23/25 tentative partially represented carried forward, not rechecked. Earlier restricted-area and serial-scope deferrals retained. Queue OPEN: additional high-value omissions found; next bounded screen of Sacred Valley regional routes, highland heritage and significant festivals, no quotas. Existing scores and two unassessed preserved. Coordinates approximate; schedules and current access unverified.
